Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The stationarity variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 406 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 473 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the stationarity decomposition. By minimality, the element a^406 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 473-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in stationarity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 453 and the invariant I(A,B) = 372 mod 3.

For the inductive step, we decompose the stationarity structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 900.
